function EnableESP(...)
	if v then
		return;
	end;
	v = true;
	for x, E in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		createESP(E);
	end;
	task[s6(799187 - 857328)](function(...)
		while v do
			pcall(updateESP);
			task[s6(346591 - 404399)](R);
		end;
	end);
end;
function DisableESP(...)
	v = false;
	for x, E in pairs(O) do
		removeESP(x);
	end;
	O = {};

function ClearAura(...)
	for x, E in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if E and E[s6(-152430 + 93946)] then
			E:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
function ApplyAura(x, ...)
	ClearAura();
	if not x then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	local E = AURA_DEFS[x];
	if not E then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	ActiveAura = x;
	local K = player[s6(-628202 + 569815)];
	if not K then
		return;
	end;
	local z = K:FindFirstChild(s6(981389 - 1039241));
	if not z then
		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		local x = Instance[s6(732581 + -790492)](s6(-37980 - 20416));
		x[s6(441351 + -499585)] = s6(-303540 - (-245513));
		x[s6(-993202 - (-935370))] = s6(379871 - 437986);
		x[s6(830493 + -888640)] = E[s6(-285530 - (-227383))];
		x[s6(-433104 - (-375117))] = E[s6(-570928 + 512941)];
		x[s6(67026 + -124871)] = E[s6(-74016 + 16171)];
		x[s6(492158 - 549965)] = E[s6(-31519 - 26288)];
		x[s6(-740905 + 683055)] = E[s6(-28303 - 29547)];
		x[s6(-45466 + -12434)] = E[s6(667722 + -725622)];
		x[s6(748425 + -806759)] = E[s6(93480 + -151814)];
		x[s6(596163 + -654317)] = E[s6(382346 + -440500)];
		x[s6(949095 - 1007126)] = E[s6(384898 - 442929)];
		x[s6(-933095 - (-874611))] = z;
		table[s6(-331152 - (-272893))](AuraObjects, x);
		local K = Instance[s6(385561 + -443472)](s6(828260 + -886387));
		K[s6(-500149 - (-441915))] = s6(-453637 - (-395748));
		K[s6(435526 - 493673)] = E[s6(620053 - 677878)];
		K[s6(122229 + -180267)] = 936155 - 936153;
		K[s6(564124 + -622586)] = 645803 + -645788;
		K[s6(-402863 + 344379)] = z;
		table[s6(-301575 - (-243316))](AuraObjects, K);
	end);

function performExtendedSoru(x, ...)

		return;
	end;

		return;

	if M <= 716782688 % 3479523 then
		pcall(function(...)
			local E = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(s6(-413327 - (-355188))) and ReplicatedStorage[s6(230070 + -288209)]:FindFirstChild(s6(-173408 - (-115343)));

			end;
		end);
	else
		local E = math[s6(-86491 + 28060)](M / (680268 + -679368));
		local t = ((x - z))[s6(218558 - 276830)];
		for x = -188457 - (-188458), E, 983172 - 983171 do
			local E = math[s6(993197 + -1051134)](x * (-993210 + 994110), M);

				end;

		end;
	end;
end;

		return false;
	end;
	return true;
end;

function getGameStat(x, ...)

	end;

end;
